12731: function hlgb916HubSummary(range){
12732:   let arr=hlgb916HubWeekEntries(range),ins=arr.filter(e=>e.flow==="Entrada"),outs=arr.filter(e=>e.flow==="Saída"),inTotal=ins.reduce((a,e)=>a+(+e.value||0),0),outTotal=outs.reduce((a,e)=>a+(+e.value||0),0);
12733:   let cashIn=ins.filter(e=>["pix","dinheiro"].includes(hlgb916Norm(e.method))).reduce((a,e)=>a+(+e.value||0),0),checksIn=ins.filter(e=>hlgb916Norm(e.method)==="cheque").reduce((a,e)=>a+(+e.value||0),0);
12734:   let hardCash=0,strictCheck=0,flexCheck=0;outs.forEach(e=>{let ms=hlgb916HubEntryMethods(e).map(hlgb916Norm),hasCheck=ms.includes("cheque"),hasCash=ms.includes("pix")||ms.includes("dinheiro");if(hasCheck&&!hasCash)strictCheck+=+e.value||0;else if(hasCheck&&hasCash)flexCheck+=+e.value||0;else hardCash+=+e.value||0});
12735:   let checkGap=Math.max(0,strictCheck-checksIn),remainingChecks=Math.max(0,checksIn-strictCheck),flexCovered=Math.min(flexCheck,remainingChecks),cashNeed=hardCash+Math.max(0,flexCheck-flexCovered),cashBalance=cashIn-cashNeed;
12736:   let realizedIn=ins.filter(e=>e.status==="Realizado").reduce((a,e)=>a+(+e.value||0),0),realizedOut=outs.filter(e=>e.status==="Realizado").reduce((a,e)=>a+(+e.value||0),0);
12737:   return {arr,ins,outs,inTotal,outTotal,balance:inTotal-outTotal,cashIn,checksIn,hardCash,strictCheck,flexCheck,checkEligible:strictCheck+flexCheck,checkGap,cashNeed,cashBalance,realizedIn,realizedOut};
